Participating in the state contributing system, it deposits its contribution in the individual pension system account and reports its share of the individual pension system's contribution to the Centre for Retirement, and the Centre for Retirement is also able to account for state contributions, allowing the public to contribute to state contributions and deposit 25 per cent of the state contribution into the sub account. The contributions will not be deducted from the tax matrix. Instead, the state contribution of 25 per cent of the contributor's contribution will be paid to the participant's lower account of the state contribution system. The maximum state contribution to a attendee is limited to 25 per cent of the annual gross minimum wage. As of July 1st, if we assume that this number is $940.50, it's $11,000 per year, and the state contribution is around $2,900 per year. As in the current system, pension investment funds and state contributions will not be taxed, only on income. The state contribution will be gradually granted the right to remain in the system for a long time. State contributions and benefits from the third year will be granted 15 per cent after the sixth year, 35 per cent after the sixth year, 60 per cent after the tenth year, and 100 per cent in retirement. The total amount, paid by employers for incentives of institutional-based participation in the system, and a reduction of the premiums paid under personal insurance, is increased from 10 per cent to 15 per cent of the gross fee. Employer-sponsored contracts have a maximum amount of time reduced from five to seven years. In pension funds, it is possible to invest in precious minerals and other alternative financial vehicles. The foundation is extended until 31/12/2015, which allows pensions for pensions in the polls and commercial companies to be transferred to the individual pension system, and the issues related to tax practices are clear. Process costs are reduced by removing the obligation to notarize the Fund's internal charter. An arrangement for the collection fee collected by the Capital Market Board for pension investment funds has been paralleled with the equity investment funds. The cost of auditing pension funds is reduced. Participants are allowed to remove some of their savings from the system without gaining pension. According to the weight of the verb committed by the individual pension vehicle, the license is also allowed to be revoked, as well as to temporarily halt the sales authority. In other parts of the bill, amendments are envisioned in the legislation that regulates the working procedures and basics of this sector in order to strengthen the financial structure and ensure a more effective risk management in companies operating in the insurance sector. However, restructuring and reorganising the security account's work and functions are intended to include the Union of Turkish Insurance and Reaccessors' pension companies. Today, the insurance premium provided by the 17 million-population Dutch insurance industry is $110 billion, while our 75 million-population country's premium in insurance is only $8 billion. The amount of premiums must be increased at least six to seven times as soon as possible. At the moment, 15 pension companies are operating on this issue, and two additional ones have arrived. Currently, there are 2 million 794,542 participants, and as the last year, this percentage has increased by 6 per cent compared to last year. As of 1 June, the total funding of participants is 16 billion dollars 458 million liras, and our increase from last year is 15 percent. The price of funding per person is 5,500,000 liras. The system was legalized on March 28, 2001, and since it came in two years later, it is becoming a new pension, and so far only 4,743 people have received pension or collective money. The average savings of retired participants is $43,600, while 75 per cent of pensioners receive pension payments at $17,800. Arrangements are being made to strengthen the financial system and enable risk management over the sector. In particular, it is authorized to increase and reduce guarantees by 50 per cent, considering the firm's finances and economic conditions, which ensure fast decisions can be made, especially in times of crisis. Private guarantees are being introduced for companies to be used for liquidation and bankruptcy. It is possible to transfer the management of the portfolio of companies within the fiscal constitution to the Trust Account. The undersecretary's membership in international occupational organisations and sharing information are being organised. The identity and address sharing system is also open to banks as well as the insurance company and the Trust Account. The tasks and functions of the Trust Account are redefined to include promotion, mandatory insurance control and contribution to the educational centre. The Insurance Centre for Information and Surveillance is being restructured by strengthening its structure. The Union of Turkish Insurance and Reaccessors is also in place of pension companies, and the union structure of the Union of Turkish Insurance and Reaccessors is being re-established, and its name is renamed the association of Turkey's insurance, resuscitation and pension companies. The application reduces sentences, which are thought to be heavy according to the verb. Another concern is the creation of the individual capital system, which has not yet been regulated in Turkey and has not yet been institutionalised, and the aim is to facilitate access to the funding of early stage and technology-oriented enterprises by offering some incentives to individual accession investors. The individual accession investor represents the capital that the individual accession investor transfers individual presence, experience and savings to initiatives in the process of beginning or development; the individual accession capital represents the capital that the individual accession investor transfers to companies. Creating a new financial instrument for early companies, which is suffering from access to financing with the bill, the creation of a certain behavioral culture and ethical rules and boosting professionalism in this market, the institutionalisation of individual accession capital, the institutionalisation of the financial market for entrepreneurs, the attracting of state support and individual accession capital investments. The bill stipulates that individual accession investors from the Treasury Undersecretary's Office, who have a license to apply by the end of 2017, will be partnered with anonymous companies, while 75 per cent of the capital they place in their annual statements as an investment reduction to individual accession investors, the issue is expected to be reduced from profits and assets. One innovation in the draft is the possibility of exporting rent certificates by the Treasury Undersecretary to contribute to financial stability through diversification of foreign financing sources and opportunity for alternative financing sources. The first lease certificate was issued by Shell Corporation in 1990. Rental certificates are a rapidly growing market. The total volume of rent certificates exported to global markets in 2012 is 170 billion dollars. Malaysia, Saudi Arabia, United Arab Emirates, Indonesia, Qatar, Bahrain and Kuwait are the main exporters in the market. Outside these countries, Western governments and companies have also exported in this market. Britain and France are making legal arrangements for the transfer of a rent certificate. Turkey's participation in this market is important for diversification of foreign financing sources. A rent certificate issued by the Treasury will be an indicator for private sector companies in our country and will contribute to the deepening of this market. When Istanbul is considered to be an international financial centre, deepening the rent certificate market in our country will be a positive development. There are at least three sides to a standard rent certificate export mechanism: the first side, the borrower; the second, the investors; the third, the property rental companies. The borrower will first form an asset rental company. The assets will be sold to the asset rental company at a later date to be repossessed at a certain price, while these assets will be rehired from the asset rental company. Ownership rental companies will export the bonds of those assets to investors, collect the necessary funds, and give them to the lender as a price for the sale of their assets. The borrower will pay rent costs to the asset rental company with certain periods, and the asset rental company will deliver these costs to those with a lease certificate, and the cash flow will be provided. At the end of the deadline, the borrower will get his assets back from the asset rental company, which will be distributed to the principal rent holders. In an effort to improve efficiency in the application on agricultural insurance, it is intended to clarify its corporate identity by specifying the legal personality of the Agricultural Insurance Pool and to provide collateral with insurance companies if the Pool is seen as appropriate by the board of directors and the conditions for risk management are necessary. tbmm-2012-06-12sit06spe0207par0141	Thank you, Mr. President. Mr. President, I have made a commitment to the first chapter of the First Section of the Bill of Law, where there will be amendments to Articles of the Law and Law under the Law, with the Savings and Investment System of Individual Retirement Act of 271 rows. I hereby salute your high council and our beloved nation. Our government's main goal is to ensure that Istanbul enters one of the world's largest financial centers in 2023. One of the issues discussed by our government to achieve this goal is the individual pension system. As of 1 June 2012, the system reached $16,459 million in funding, while the number of participants reached 2 million 794,500. Although it has made great progress in a short time, considering the young population of our country, we can easily see that this system has great potential for development. In fact, we aim to promote and spread the individual pension system through the amendments of the law. Proliferation of the system will serve the goal of increasing domestic savings. With the increase of domestic savings, financing will extend and financing opportunities will be diversified. With the continuation of financial stability and extended deadlines, Turkey will become more attractive to investors. The most important change in the individual pension system is actually in the application of public incentives. Tax incentives are already offered in the individual pension system to encourage participants to join the system. According to this, the amount of contributions paid by participants are allowed to be downloaded from the tax machine within certain limits, no taxation is made in the investment phase, and the amount of time in the system to leave the system depends on the amount of time it takes. Although it has the ability to save money, such as housewives, the proportion of non-tax collectors or participants who are not able to reduce their contribution is about 65 per cent, although they are taxpayers due to operational difficulties. This also comes under the obligation to change the incentive structure in the individual pension system when the total income tax cuts, including capital money, are added during the separation from the system. In this scope, regulations are being provided to ensure that the bill and the individual pension system's incentive structure can be used both by larger masses and that the income situation is more equitable. We are changing the system of incentives that are already implemented with the bill, both to enable more people to benefit from public incentives, to give the larger masses this advantage, and to give everyone the equal advantage. Instead of a tax reduction advantage in the new incentive system brought in with the bill, it is an application of state contribution. So the income tax will be transferred to a system that everyone can benefit from, whether it's a taxpayer or not. In this scope, 25 per cent of the contributions paid by participants to the individual pension system will be paid to the lower individual accounts as a state contribution. However, there will be an upper limit, the amount of state contribution available within a year will be limited to 25 per cent of the gross minimum wage per year. The main goal of the individual pension system is to increase the level of income for individuals during their retirement period. After all, the system is a long-term system. Instigating staying in the system is also the main goal. In this direction, state contributions paid for the participant also have gradual merit practices. According to the bill, the state will not be able to touch its contribution for the first three years. At the end of the third year, it will be possible to qualify for 15 per cent, 35 per cent at the end of the 6th year, and 60 per cent in the 10th year. When individuals receive pension rights from the individual pension system, they will have access to all state contributions and benefits. With all of these changes, it is planned that people who have less income in the individual pension system or who have so far no tax advantage should be included in a system where everyone takes equal advantage, so that the system will be spread to the bottom, and hopefully the bill will come out of here. I, on this occasion, salute your high council again.

Dear lawmakers, with this bill, the tax reduction in the individual pension arrangement is being waived to implement state contribution. In the current arrangement, those who put bonuses into the individual pension system may be able to download it from the income tax matrix. If a person's income is subject to 15 per cent income tax, he can reduce 15 per cent of the premium he has invested, and if he is in a 35 per cent tax tranche, he can reduce 35 per cent of the bonus. The application of state contributions is 25 per cent. The new arrangement provides an advantage for the sub-income group, bringing a disadvantage to the upper income group. In the current arrangement, however, the person will receive a tax reduction immediately, while the person will receive a state contribution only when he retires. The current application pays a stoppage of 25 per cent of the total bonus amount when a person retires or leaves the system after being subject to the exception of the income tax. In the new system, the person is not entitled to a state contribution without retirement, nor can he benefit from 25 per cent of the exception. In addition, in the new system, the person will be entitled to 15 per cent of the state contribution by three years, 60 per cent by ten years, and 100 per cent when he retires. So when a person pays a ten-year bonus in the new system, one will not benefit from the exception of income tax; two from the exception of the 25 per cent income tax on the bonus; three from the 40 per cent share of the state contribution. The new system contains regulations in favour of insurance companies rather than people. 60 per cent of the government's contribution to the person is returned to the individual, while 40 per cent remain in the company. These regulations encourage insurance companies rather than individual pension and social security. The 31st article of the bill also brings health and education investments to health and educational investments, provided the foreign loans provided by the public administration of the facilities are taken over before the deadline for investment and services carried out under the Act 3996. The bill refers to the tender of large-scale projects that are vital to our country and the purpose of providing construction goods and services to companies who have received the project as part of the project, as well as the exception of VAT for subsidising health and education facilities in this scope. Hospitals are built by universities as well as by the Ministry of Health. The private sector also builds hospitals. If the aim of this arrangement is to encourage big and important investments and reduce the financial burden, we think it would be more appropriate for the VAT exception to cover all major investments. In Article 32 of the bill, the export certificate is organised. This statement may lead to the exclusion of all business and transactions of the Undersecretary of the Treasury under the Law on Public Contraction and the Law on State Contraction. The scope of the exception is very clear in the Public Contraction Act, and the addition of all jobs and transactions to the lease certificate in Article (11) gives the impression that this arrangement has some other purpose. Public Contraction Act 4734 was adopted on January 4, 2002, and it was intended to ensure efficient use of the Law, of the tenders, of transparency, competition, equal treatment, credibility, privacy, public control, resources. The law has also taken into account the norms of international organisations, such as the World Trade Organization, which are in harmony with the European Union's legislation. The 53rd Article of the Law has been established by the Public Contractorship Agency, which has a legal administrative and financial autonomy of its public corporate personality to carry out the duties given by this law. So it's not right to view this as just the Public Contraction Act, it's better to consider the matter as a whole with the Public Contraction Agency. tbmm-2012-06-12sit10spe0403par0062	Thank you, Mr. President. I salute everyone at this hour of the morning. One of the created perceptions is that Turkey's total debt on economic policy has fallen. Indeed, when we look at it, we see the debt, which is fundamental to Maastricht's criteria, is reduced by the public debt divided by the illegitimate nation. This rate, which was 78 per cent the following of the 2001 crisis, and this rate, close to the 80°, is now down to 39 per cent today, and is estimated to reach 32 per cent in 2014 under the medium-term programme. However, we can't say with the same logic that the total debt of the economy is reduced. Because the public debt is reduced due to its extra-interest policies, but the economy's dynamic of creating debt continues. As long as a country has a structural deficit of current transactions and finances its current transaction deficit with creative pens, its external debt will not be reduced to a national amount. So, what happens when public debt, public foreign debt, is reduced to a multi-billionth, but who finances the total debt on the economy's debt-making dynamic, and who carries it? Well, that's where the private sector comes in. During the AKP administrations, the foreign debt of the private sector was nearly 8 ▪. Think of the economy as a balloon. As the public tightens its debt with its extracurricular policies, the other side of the balloon swells as the public tightens its debt, the private sector accepts the debt because the economy's debt-making dynamic continues. The rise in private sector debt triggers another vulnerability in the economy: private sector exchange positions deficits. It's usually not much of a discussion, and it's covered up. In the 2001 crisis, we paid off the exchange positions with a very heavy bill in the banking sector. What were the banks doing before 2001? They were in debt in foreign currency, and they came and lent the Treasury money in dollars. Their assets were in dollars, their obligations were in exchange. So, they were exposed to exchange positions. After the 2001 crisis, the exchange went too far, so many banks in the banking sector went down because of this exchange position deficit, and we paid the price. The BDDK was then established, and with BDK's recording systems and registrations and restrictions, the exchange position gaps became almost zero. However, this time, the real sector's exchange position deficits have surfaced. See, the real sector's exchange position deficit today is 126 billion dollars. In other words, the real sector's assets in currency are less than $126 billion in foreign currency. So the real sector is at great risk of a courtship. This makes the real sector incredibly vulnerable to international developments, especially to the risk of courtship. It's obvious that when currency prices go too high and there's a little bit left around here, real sector companies are in danger of going down. Thus, there is a possibility that the relationship established in 2001, and the crisis that emerged over him, will occur in an inverse way this time. In 2001, the banks sank due to a lack of currency position, and this deficit led them to the real sector through the credit channels. Now there's a chance that the real sector will fall into crisis and the real sector crisis will again break balance sheets in the banking sector via the credit channel. So, dear friends, the need for Turkey's financing is increasing and the lack of current transactions, and the fact that this financial deficit is being met with debt-crushing pens and that this is being met with the real sector creates a serious new vulnerability in the economy, and this vulnerability is a new, important, powerful vulnerability in addition to the vulnerability that I have expressed in front of the Turkish economy in my previous talks.
